\title{Melanie-MASTERS (human masters) — Catalan}

\maketitle

\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, White: \texttt{Ne5}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{Ne5} (\texttt{f3e5}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0023\#2595} (ply 22) [structural distance $d$=57, QAP obj=69]: the master played \texttt{Re1}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Rf1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Rf1}; cross-type roles: our \emph{N}b1 as their \emph{R}a1, our \emph{P}a2 as their \emph{B}c1; transferred move \texttt{f1e1}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0012\#2883} (ply 23) [structural distance $d$=55, QAP obj=60]: the master played \texttt{Nd5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Nf3} plays the role of that game's \emph{Nf3}; cross-type roles: our \emph{b}e7 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{B}c1 as their \emph{P}a7; transferred move \texttt{f3h4}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{lichess\_elite\_2024-09\_p0010\#1215} (ply 21) [structural distance $d$=52, QAP obj=52]: the master played \texttt{Qd6}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Qd1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Qd1}; cross-type roles: our \emph{b}e7 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{q}d8 as their \emph{N}b8; transferred move \texttt{d1d3}. Synthesis: no retrieved analog transferred to \texttt{Ne5} -- the \texttt{search} selector chose it on value (material / mate); panels (b)--(d) show the strongest nearby analogies.}
\label{fig:thought-m12}
\end{figure}
